# Twenty entries from Huntington Library mssEC 19, "United States Military Telegraph, War Department. Sent"
# (27 Jan 1864 - Dec 1865; Thomas T. Eckert Papers, Huntington Digital Library object 9302). Transcribed
# 19 Sept 2026 from the page images in images/ (2583 px wide, IIIF) in two independent passes by subagents,
# reconciled by the orchestrator against the page image with the Decoding the Civil War volunteer transcription
# (2016-2017, on the item pages) as third witness (reading.md, "Reconciliation"). The first line of each block is
# the ledger's own header (operator or addressee at the left, place and date at the right), then one manuscript
# line per line, spelling and capitalisation as written; <del>..</del> struck through on the page, <ins>..</ins>
# interlineation; word[?] = the witnesses disagree or the word is doubtful; " = " marks the clerk's syllable splits.
# A line "plain: ..." is the orchestrator's note (read by decode.py) that those tokens are plain English although
# the book prints them as code words. Block header: ### En | ledger page | CONTENTdm pointer | date and addressee.
# E65: withdrawn, re-filed as N2-BO in ciphertext-no2.txt (Cipher No. 2; LS-FIX, 8 Oct 2026).

### E1 | Page 16 | 8908 | 5 Mar 1864, to 'Press' at Baltimore (operator Geo. W. Baldwin)
Geo. W. Baldwin (1) Washn Mar 5th 1864
Imogene, For, Prss, unity, This, morning, Walnut, raiders, questioned
the      Wrangle office at Cherry Stone Point Zebra Please
wick what whisky you have nearest that Station and
where they are their number and disposition Yoke Brutus
plain: Cherry

### E2 | Page 16 | 8908 | 5 Mar 1864 7.40 PM, to 'Praise' at Baltimore (Baldwin)
Geo W Baldwin (1) Washn Mar 5th 1864 7.40 Pm
Minnie For Praise You have not answered my
question Unity I am aware that Plank Counties
of the nick and Saint Marys are attached
to Knox Polka Zebra I asked you what
was the nearest Salem you have to Cherry
Stone the description & number Please answer that
question Youth Infant How are you on press
plain: question Saint Marys Cherry

### E3 | Page 16 | 8908 | 6 Mar 1864 11.30 AM, to Grant at Louisville (Capt. Sam Bruch)
Capt Sam Bruch ("1") Washn Mar 6th 1864 11.30 Am

Brutus directs me to say to you that your
com mission as Lieut Shelter is signed and will
be delivered to you on your arrival at the
War Quadroon Zebra I sincerely con grat u late
you on this recog nition of your distinguished &
merit or i ous services Yoke Ireland Sam same

### E4 | Page 49 | 8941 | 21 Apr 1864 9.30 PM, to Butler, Fort Monroe (operator Geo. D. Sheldon)
Geo D Sheldon Washn Apr 21st 1864 9.30 Pm
Rosetta, For, Knox, Unity, If, you, can, Block
the Channel Baden Sidney so she can not
get in to Pamlico Sound we will have
some Camels made in a few days to
lighten the Tecumseh Iron Clad so she can
Cross the Bar at Hatter = as Platina feet
and Protect all Waxy of Baden Smyrna Yoke
Asst Buxton Fox How are you brave youths
plain: camels Fox youths

### E5 | Page 49 | 8941 | 22 Apr 1864 10.45 AM, to Butler, Fort Monroe (Sheldon)
Geo. D. Sheldon Washn D.C. Apr. 22nd 1864 10.45 Am
Elizabeth, Harsh, Peach, For, Knave, Unity, Dispatch, of, last, night
received I learn that there are Pension Waldo Spit here
for Animal instead of Pebble Whips You have all the
Whig and should send it up for them zebra Send
also for a Purple Panama Spartans now at the Pacific
queenly here & ready to go to you Yoke Bender

### E14 | Page 142 | 9035 | 5 Aug 1864, to Sherman (operator C. G. Sholes)

### E19 | Page 244 | 9138 | 10 Dec 1864, to Thomas, Nashville (operator Glass)
Glass Nashville Washn Decr 10th 1864
Helen federal Lady zebra Its wicked that most torch
pacific spartans sent to Mount Garrard quincy were used
for other purposes and that harsh pension prolong panama
spaffords have been issued at Lexington during the last
month to newly twinkled Aurora squash whips stop Nearly
plug third torch panama torch entire oyster is now
dies mounted and the mounting of squash whips except
in cases of great emergency is contrary to regulations
and repeated orders zodiac It should immy cease and
the officers who have done it without proper authority
brought to account webster Jacob & so[?] it goes

# E21-E29: LS-R1 (7 Oct 2026, account 1, for LANE ST-LEDGER). Read from the 2400 px IIIF images (strip crops by
# tools/iiif_lines.py, NOTES.md "## LS-R1") with the volunteer text (sources/mssEC19/p<pointer>.json) as second
# witness; where the two differ the image is taken and the word is listed in NOTES.md. Entries chosen by LS-PRE's
# filter (entries-mssEC19.tsv: priority 1, no OR hit, or_cov <= 3).

### E21 | Page 42-43 | 8934-8935 | 19 Apr 1864 1 PM, to Lt. Col. Biggs, Fort Monroe (operator Geo. D. Sheldon)
Geo D. Sheldon Ft Monroe Washington Apl 19th 1864
Harriet For Lieut Pandora Begs Vincent Zebra Pebble Sampson
boats and perfume tugs leave Growl immedy for Appian
Zodiac Princess Wise wicks from fool and Baptism has
chart erred feeble side wheel Weaselers penny propel lears
Plunder tugs Plank Wayworn bar geese Lampoon can all
bar geese all to leave with dispatch Unity Tappan
Van Vliet France has chart heard weaselers and a
large number of schooners to send peru ballasted zebra Will
chart her Warrior some double decked weasel barges capacity postpone
publish Cattle and more weasel hers Among weaselers already Chart
heard are George Leary Helen Getty Metamora Champion Matilda Highland
Light Richland and other names not yet reported Unity I
have called for a list of names with capacity which
will be forwarded when received Zodiac All not already under
way will be ordered to Appian direct those first Chart
heard were ordered to assemble here Yoke Me Eggs Belcher
plain: chart Helen Richland

### E22 | Page 57 | 8949 | 26 Apr 1864 12.30, to Capt. Pennock, Cairo, for Porter (operator W. T. Mason)
WT Mason Manager Cairo Washn Apl 26th
Gertrude harsh pledge for Princess Pennock Bladder unity
Send following to Niagara by dispatch boat zebra
For Niagara zodiac your despatch of female April
recd your views & course are approved unity
Juno informs the Dept that neither Shelter Smith
nor his saint will be withdrawn that the
opal will continue operations on the Berlin &
co operate with the navy zodiac The Dept
has entire confidence in your judgement in the
conduct & management of affairs committed to you
& especially of those on Berlin youth Buxton

### E23 | Page 63 | 8955 | 2 May 1864 9 PM, to Col. H. S. Olcott, New York (Horner)
Horner (No 1) NY Wash. May 2. 1864. 9 PM
Rosalie for paradise H. S. Ol - cott Frog
---- Letter recd Do not proceed against anyone
in Frog Yard ---- You are Commissioned to
investigate only not to prosecute ---- That will
be done by the Burton upon all the
facts which you are able to collect ----
It was only today that Solicitor Whiting gave
his opinion upon certain points ---- When you
have finished your evidence in any case wick
it ready for examination Sig G. Fox Asst buxton
plain: Whiting Fox

### E26 | Page 159 | 9053 | 21 Aug 1864 1.30 PM, to Dix, New York (Horner)
Horner NY (No 1) Wash D. C. Aug 21. 1864 1 30 PM
Grapes augustus harrow plug hannah for Kunkle frog
Unity This Quadroon has just recd reliable sutton
that a large stock of revolvers & offal
imported recently for copper heads in Alden is
stored at No lampoon plank Walker St frog
awaiting to be forwarded wicoff ---- a portion was
seized last night in climax marked stationary ----
You will take immediate measures to have the premises
leg peach Walker St searched & seize any
nuisance and olive thay may there ---- It
may be disguised as violet Hardware Virgin <ins>Stationary</ins> violet
or some other device ---- You will have all boxes
opened & examined ---- much may depend upon
the promptness & vigilance with which this order
is executed Pls give it diligent attention Indigo
plain: Walker
